硬件外部下拉; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F11/F12/F13/F14/F15/F16/F17/F18/F19/F25/F27</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F19</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>1V8转1V8_EQ用于EQ6供电，现选择负载开关方案。 1V8 to 1V8_EQ is used for the EQ6 power supply, now choose the load switch scheme. </td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>采用TPS22918负载开关，将VCC1V8电源转换为VCC1V8_EQ，给EQ6供电。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 The TPS22918 load switch chip is used to convert the VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ to power EQ6. 问题：文档声明板上有哪些 CAN 相关模块，回答时能否用网表确认其具体连接？
参考答案：HSI 硬件架构包含 CAN/CAN FD 电路；具体器件和网络连接应以 EDF 检索结果为准，文档能力声明与网表拓扑需分别标注来源。
实际回答：根据提供的证据，以下是对您问题的回答，按子问题组织，并包含来源说明和缺失信息。 ### 子问题1：文档中声明了板上有哪些CAN相关模块？ **回答：** 根据文档（来源：`600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`）中的证据，板上的CAN相关模块包括 **CAN0、CAN1、CAN2、CAN3** 四个CAN通道。这些模块在网表中通过对应的网络名称（如CAN0H、CAN1H、CAN2H、CAN3H等）体现。 **支撑证据：** - The overall hardware architecture is described as follows:整体系统由摄像头板（CAM）和主板（MB）两部分组成。The overall system is composed of two parts: camera board (CAM) and mainboard (MB).前向摄像头采集道路图像后通过数字接口输出视频数据。The forward camera collects road images and outputs video data through the digital interface.数字视频数据通过线缆传输到主板上的视频处理器EYEQ6进行视频处理。The digital video data is transmitted to the video processor EYEQ6 on the mainboard through cables for video processing.EYEQ6处理视频数据，输出路况信息。EYEQ6 processes video data and outputs road condition information.路况信息通过SPI口传送给MCU进行处理，主板通过CAN对外传送路况信息。Road condition information is transmitted to MCU through SPI interface for processing, and the mainboard transmits road condition information externally through CAN.处理结果通过CAN发出，包括对车的主动安全控制信号以及给驾驶员的报警信号。The processing result is sent out through CAN, including the active safety control signal for the car and the alarm signal for the driver.图 1 系统工作方式Fig 1 System operating block硬件框图 Hardware block diagram图 2 系统框图Fig 2 system block功能安全分解相关模块模块电路 Module circuitKL30电源管理电路 KL30 supply management circuit | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F13</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电。VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电。VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。 VCCIV8 is used to power EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH.VCC1V1 is used to power EQ6,DDR. VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, now choose LP87702 PMIC scheme. </td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 采用LP87702芯片，将VCC3V3转换为VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50，其中VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1输出电压和使能。用MCU的P12.1引脚复位该芯片。 LP87702 chip is used to convert VCC3V3 into VCC1V8, VCC1V1 and VCC50, in which VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR and FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6 and DDR power supply, and VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supplyP15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1 output voltage and enable it. Reset the chip with P12.1 pin of the MCU. </td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F02/F05/F10</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>对EQ6和LPDDR4周边PCB温度进行监控。AOU需求MCU监控EQ6芯片内结温，确保EQ6工作安全。AOU需求EQ6监控CAM的工作温度，要求温度监控功能达到ASIL-B等级，确保EQ6系统的功能安全。 The PCB temperature around EQ6 and LPDDR4 was monitored. AOU requires MCU to monitor the junction temperature inside EQ6 chip to ensure the safety of EQ6. AOU requires EQ6 to monitor the operating temperature of the CAM, and the temperature monitoring function is required to reach ASIL-B level to ensure the functional safety of the EQ6 system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td> </td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>EQ6温度采样 EQ6-L内部内置了两个温度传感器，并通过外部SPI接口实时发送给MCU，硬件设计可以支持MCU对EQ6结温的监控。 1, EQ6 temperature sampling EQ6-l has two internal temperature sensors, which are sent to the MCU in real time through the external SPI interface. The hardware design can support the MCU to monitor the junction temperature of EQ6. NTC温度采样 硬件设计使用温敏电阻NTC（NCU15XH103F6SRC）采集EQ6/LPDDR4周边环境温度，并通过MCU进行ADC采样和实时监控。NTC温度监控范围：-40~150℃，监控精度±3℃@-40~150℃，MCU的ADC为10bit，即1024个量阶，使得监测精度可以达到3.2mV，电压采集范围0-3.3V。用MCU的AN7引脚采样。 2.NTC temperature sampling In hardware design, the temperature sensor NTC (NCU15XH103F6SRC) is used to collect the ambient temperature of EQ6/LPDDR4, and the ADC sampling and real-time monitoring are carried out by MCU. NTC temperature monitoring range: -40-150℃, monitoring accuracy ±3℃@-40-150℃, MCU ADC is 10bit, that is, 1024 quantity order, so that the monitoring accuracy can reach 3.2mV, voltage acquisition range 0-3.3V. The AN7 pin of the MCU is used for sampling. CAM温度采样 CAM的IMX-728内置了两个温度传感器，温度测量范围是：-40~125℃，温度监控精度为±5℃@-40~125℃。EYEQ6可以通过I2C接口（EQ6L的I2C_0_SCLK和I2C_0_SDA引脚）实时读取IMX-728的工作温度，并进行状态监控和异常诊断。 3.CAM temperature sampling CAM IMX-728 built-in two temperature sensors, temperature measurement range is: -40~125℃, temperature monitoring accuracy of ±5℃@-40~125℃. EYEQ6 can read the operating temperature of the IMX-728 in real time through the I2C interface (I2C_0_SCLK and I2C_0_SDA pins of EQ6L), and perform condition monitoring and anomaly diagnosis. </td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F08/F09</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统需求需要板内电源电压进行ADC采样和异常监控，要求功能安全达到ASIL-B等级，确保EQ6系统工作安全。 The system requirements require the on-board power supply voltage for ADC sampling and anomaly monitoring, and the functional safety is required to reach ASIL-B level to ensure the working safety of EQ6 system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>EQ6系统电源电压采样 系统需求需要MCU对LP87702、TPS62872、SA23002、TPS22918和摄像头端的电压VCC3V3_EQ，VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC3V3_CAM，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM进行ADC采样和异常监控。其中VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM电压范围未超量程对电压直接采样。VCC3V3_EQ和VCC3V3_CAM经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_EQ用AN2引脚采样，VCC1V8用AN3引脚采样，VCC1V1用AN4引脚采样，VCC1V8_EQ用AN5引脚采样，VCC3V3_CAM用AN12引脚采样，VCC1V1_CAM用AN13引脚采样，VCC1V8_CAM用AN14引脚采样，VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR用AN18引脚采样，VCC0V75用AN19引脚采样。 EQ6 system power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the voltages VCC3V3_EQ, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC3V3_CAM, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM for the LP87702, TPS62872, SA23002, TPS22918, and camera-side devices. Among these voltages, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM have voltage ranges within the measurement range and are directly sampled. VCC3V3_EQ and VCC3V3_CAM are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_EQ is sampled using AN2, VCC1V8 using AN3, VCC1V1 using AN4, VCC1V8_EQ using AN5, VCC3V3_CAM using AN12, VCC1V1_CAM using AN13, VCC_1V8_CAM using AN14, 0V75_MIPI_DDR using AN18, and VCC0V75 using AN19. CAN&ETH电源电压采样 系统需求MCU对LP87702、TPS22918的输出电压VCC3V3_ETH，VCC50进行ADC采样和异常监控，VCC3V3_ETH和VCC50经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_ETH用AN8引脚采样，VCC50用AN1引脚采样。 CAN&ETH power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the output voltages VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 from the LP87702 and TPS22918. Both VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_ETH is sampled using AN8, and VCC50 is sampled using AN1.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F07/F25/F28/F31</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>ADAS产品支持KL15唤醒、CAN唤醒（平台预留）和以太网唤醒（平台预留）三种唤醒方式，当唤醒信号有效时直接使能一级电源，MCU开始工作并启动ADAS产品，并且MCU可以通过MCU_WAKE_HOLD_P12.0信号辅助使能一级电源，实现off delay功能。 ADAS products support KL15 wake up, CAN wake up（Platform reservation），ETH wake up（Platform reservation）, three wake up modes. When the wake up signal is effective, the first-level power supply will be directly enabled. MCU starts to work and start the ADAS product, and the MCU can enable the first-level power supply through the MCU_WAKE_HOLD_P12.0 signal assist to achieve the off delay function.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL15唤醒 KL15 Wake Up KL15分成两路信号： The KL15 signal divides into two signals: 一路经过防反二极管和分压电阻后用于使能一级电源LN10046，当KL15为逻辑高时有效，一级电源开始工作。并且MCU得电启动后会使能MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚，通过防反接二极管辅助使能一级电源并在唤醒源掉电后实现OFF DELAY功能，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 One way passes through an anti-reverse diode and two divider resistors , used to enable the EN pin of the primary power supply LN10046, effective when the KL15 is logically high, the primary power supply starts working. The MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin will be enabled after the MCU is powered on, and the primary power supply will be enabled through the anti-reverse diode assist, and the function of OFF DELAY will be performed after the wake source is powered off to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks. 另一路通过防反接二极管和100K,1%、10K,1%分压电路，用于MCU的ADC采样进行是否有效判断。 用到的引脚网络是WKUP_ACQ，对应MCU的AN0引脚。 The other way through the anti-reverse diode and 100K,1%, 20K,1% voltage divider circuit, used for MCU ADC sampling to determine whether effective. The pin network used is WKUP_ACQ, which corresponds to the AN0 pin of the MCU. CAN特定帧唤醒 ADAS平台支持特定报文的CAN唤醒功能，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。 CAN通讯接口，TCAN1145配置时支持CAN特定报文唤醒，TCAN1145的VBAT引脚接至UB_PR引脚，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当CAN总线接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。INH信号通过一个防范二极管在通过100K和10K电阻分压后用MCU的AN11引脚采样。 2.CAN0 bus wake up The ADAS platform reserves the CAN wake-up function to support specific messages. When the wake-up signal is effective, the enable signal of the first-level power supply will be directly enabled, the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the entire ADAS product. CAN communication interface, TCAN1145 supports CAN specific message wake-up when configured, the VBAT pin of TCAN1145 is connected to UB_PR pin, The INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the CAN bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, which will enable the primary power and the product will start to work. The INH signal is sampled with the AN11 pin of the MCU via a guard diode after voltage splitting through 100K and 33K resistors. 3.ETH报文唤醒 ADAS产品唤醒支持ETH报文唤醒，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。ETH PHY DP83TC812R的VSLEEP引脚接至常电，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当以太网接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。 3.Flexray specific packet wakeup The wakeup of ADAS products supports the wakeup of ETH message. When the wakeup signal is effective, the enable signal of the primary power supply will be directly enabled, and the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the whole ADAS product. The VSLEEP pin of the PHY DP83TC812R is connected to the Constant Power Supply, and the INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the ETH bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, and the primary power is enabled and the product starts to work. 4.自保持唤醒 MCU_WKUP_HOLD 当CAN总线唤醒报文中断后，INH引脚电平会无效（逻辑低），此时MCU会通过MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚进入OFF DELAY功能自保持唤醒一段时间，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 4. Self keeping wake up When the CAN bus wake-up frame is interrupted, the INH pin level will be invalid (low logic), and the MCU will enter the OFF DELAY function through the MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin to keep awake for a period of time to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks.</td></tr></table>

问题：仅根据网表，3V3 过压保护的精确触发阈值是多少？
参考答案：网表只能表明连接拓扑，不能给出精确保护阈值；当前缺少对应数据手册或规格证据，应明确无法确认。
实际回答：根据提供的证据，**3V3过压保护电路的精确触发阈值无法仅从网表中获取**。网表（证据1-5）仅描述了相关网络连接（如`&3V3_EQ_ACQ`、`&3V3_ETH_ACQ`、`MCU_3V3_EQ_EN`、`MCU_3V3_ETH_EN`、`VCC3V3`），未包含任何阈值数值。 设计文档（证据6）明确给出了该阈值为 **4.03V**（当 VCC3V3 > 4.03V 时触发过压保护）。该文档来源为 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`，内容为模块F18（3V3过压保护电路）的方案描述。因此，若需精确阈值，应以设计文档为准，但网表本身不提供此信息。 **缺失信息**：无，网表不包含阈值，但文档已补充。 **证据冲突**：在检索到的冲突列表中，未发现与“3V3过压保护触发阈值”直接相关的冲突。其他冲突字段（如No.、Alarm ID、Alarm name等）不影响本问题回答。
评分正文：根据提供的证据，**3V3过压保护电路的精确触发阈值无法仅从网表中获取**。网表（证据1-5）仅描述了相关网络连接（如`&3V3_EQ_ACQ`、`&3V3_ETH_ACQ`、`MCU_3V3_EQ_EN`、`MCU_3V3_ETH_EN`、`VCC3V3`），未包含任何阈值数值。 设计文档（证据6）明确给出了该阈值为 **4.03V**（当 VCC3V3 > 4.03V 时触发过压保护）。该文档来源为 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`，内容为模块F18（3V3过压保护电路）的方案描述。因此，若需精确阈值，应以设计文档为准，但网表本身不提供此信息。 **缺失信息**：无，网表不包含阈值，但文档已补充。 **证据冲突**：在检索到的冲突列表中，未发现与“3V3过压保护触发阈值”直接相关的冲突。其他冲突字段（如No.、Alarm ID、Alarm name等）不影响本问题回答。

<table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F11/F12/F13/F14/F15/F16/F17/F18/F19/F25/F27</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table> | 9.3.11 Undervoltage Lockout (UVLO) The device has an undervoltage lockout function that disables the device if the supply voltage is too low for correct operation. The negative-going threshold of the UVLO function is 2.5 V (typical). If the supply voltage decreases below this value, the device stops switching and, if DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register, turns on the output discharge. 9.3.12 Overvoltage Lockout (OVLO) The device has an overvoltage lockout function that disables the DC/DC converter if the supply voltage is too high for correct operation. The positive-going threshold of the OVLO function is 6.3 V (typical). If the supply voltage increases above this value, the device stops switching and, if DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register, turns on the output discharge. The device automatically starts switching again - it begins a new soft-start sequence - when the supply voltage falls below 6.2 V (typical) 9.3.13 Overcurrent Protection 9.3.13.1 Cycle-by-Cycle Current Limiting If the peak inductor current increases above the high-side current limit threshold, the device turns off the high-side switch and turns on the low-side switch to ramp down the inductor current. The device only turns on the high-side switch again if the inductor current has decreased below the low-side current limit threshold. Note that because of the propagation delay of the current limit comparator, the current limit threshold in practice can be greater than the DC value specified in the Electrical Characteristics. This bit reports whether a power-bad event</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td></td><td></td><td></td><td >(output voltage too high) occurred since the last time the STATUS </td></tr> <tr><td></td><td></td><td></td><td></td><td >register was read.</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td></td><td></td><td></td><td >Ob = No power-bad overvoltage event occurred</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td></td><td></td><td></td><td >1b = A power-bad overvoltage event occurred</td></tr> </table> | During PFM-DCM operation, the device keeps the peak inductor current constant (at a level corresponding to the minimum on time of the converter) and skips pulses to regulate the output (see Figure 9-4). The switching pulses that occur during PFM-DCM operation are synchronized to the internal clock. For very small output voltages, an absolute minimum on time of approximately 50 ns reduces the switching frequency from the set value. Figure 8-5 shows the maximum switching frequency with 3.3-V and 5.5-V supplies. Use Equation 1 to calculate the output current threshold at which the device enters PFM-DCM. The user can configure the device to use either forced PWM (FPWM) mode or power save mode (PSM): In forced PwM mode, the device uses PWM-CCM at all times. In power save mode, the device uses PWM-CCM at medium and high loads, PWM-DCM at low loads, and PFM-DCM at very low loads. The transition between the different operating modes is seamless. which control the operating mode of the device. If spread-spectrum clocking is enabled, the device automatically operates in FPwM, regardless of the state of FPWMEN bit in the CONTROL1 register (see Section 9.3.9). 9.3.3PreciseEnable The Enable (EN) pin is bidirectional and has two functions: As an input, EN enables and disables the DC/DC converter in the device. · As an output, EN provides a SYSTEM_READY signal to other devices in a stacked configuration. Because there is an internal open-drain transistor connected to the EN pin, do not drive this pin directly from a low-impedance source. Instead, use a resistor to limit the current flowing into the EN pin (see Section 10). When power is first applied to the VIN pin, the device pulls the EN pin low until it has loaded its default register settings from nonvolatile memory and read the state of the VSEL, FSEL, and SYNCOUT pins. The device also pulls EN low if a fault, such as thermal shutdown or overvoltage lockout, occurs. In stacked configurations, all devices share a common enable signal, which means that the DC/DC converters in the stack cannot start to switch until all devices in the stack have completed their initialization. Similarly, a fault in one or more devices in the stack disables all converters in the stack (see Section 9.3.17). | window comparator monitors the output voltage, and the output of the comparator goes high if the output voltage is either less than 95% (typical) or greater than 105% (typical) of the nominal output voltage. The output of the window comparator is deglitched - the typical deglitch time is 40 μs - and then used to drive the open-drain PG pin. During DVS activity, when the DC/DC converter transitions from one output voltage setting to another, the output voltage can temporarily exceed the limits of the window comparator and pull the PG pin low. The device has a feature to disable this behavior. If PGBLNKDVS = 1 in the CONTROL3 register, the device ignores the output of the power-good window comparator while DvS is active. Note that the PG pin is always low, regardless of the output of the window comparator, when: · The device is in thermal shutdown. The device is disabled. The device is in undervoltage lockout.· The device is in soft start. 9.3.14.2 Secondary Device Behavior initialization, the device presets FF1 and FF2, which pulls down the PG pin and forces the device to operate in DCM. When the device completes its soft start, it resets FF2, which turns off Q1. However, in a stacked configuration, all devices share the same PG signal, and therefore the PG pin stays low until all devices in the stack have completed their soft start. When that happens, FF1 is reset and the converters operate in CCM. 9.3.15 Remote Sense The device has two pins, VOSNS and GOSNS, to remotely sense the output voltage. Remote sensing lets the converter sense the output voltage directly at the point-of-load and increases the accuracy of the output voltage regulation. 9.3.16 Thermal Warning and Shutdown The device has a two-level overtemperature detection function. If the junction temperature rises above the thermal warning threshold of 150°C (typical), the device sets the TWARN bit in the STATUS register. The device clears the TWARN bit if the user reads the STATUS register when the junction temperature is below the TWARN threshold of 130°C (typical). If the junction temperature rises above the thermal shutdown threshold of 170°C (typical), the device: · Stops switching · Pulls down the EN pin (if SINGLE = 0 in the CONTROL3 register) · Enables the output discharge (if DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register) ·Sets the TSHUT bit in the STATUS register · Pulls the PG pin low | Vin is the input voltage Vout is the output voltage.· · L is the effective inductance at the peak current level. tpd is the propagation delay of the current limit comparator (typically 50 ns). 9.3.13.2HiccupMode To enable hiccup operation, ensure that HICCUPEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register. If hiccup operation is enabled and the high-side switch current exceeds the current limit threshold on 32 consecutive switching cycles, the device: Stops switching for 128 clock cycles, after which it automatically starts switching again (it starts a new soft-start sequence) · Sets the HICCUP bit in the STATUS register Pulls the PG pin low. The PG pin stays low until the overload condition goes away and the device can start up correctly and regulate the output voltage. Note that power-good function has a deglitch circuit, which delays the rising edge of the power-good signal by 40 μs (typical). Hiccup operation continues in a repeating sequence of 32 cycles in current limit, followed by a pause of 128 cycles, followed by a soft-start attempt for as long as the output overload condition exists. The device clears the HICCUP bit if the user reads the STATUS register when the overload condition has been removed. 9.3.13.3CurrentLimitMode To enable current limit mode, ensure that HICCUPEN = 0 in the CONTROL1 register. When current limit operation is enabled, the device limits the high-side switch current cycle-by-cycle for as long as the overload condition exists. If the device limits the high-side switch current for four or more consecutive switching cycles, the device sets ILIM = 1 in the STATUS register. The device clears the ILIM bit if the user reads the STATUS register when the overload condition no longer exits. 9.3.14 Power Good (PG) The power good (PG) pin is bidirectional and has two functions: · In a standalone configuration and in the primary device of a stacked configuration, the PG pin is an open- drain output that indicates the status of the converter or stack. · In a secondary device of a stacked configuration, the PG pin is an input that indicates when the soft-start sequence is complete and all converters in the stack can change from DCM switching to CCM switching. 9.3.14.1 Standalone orPrimaryDeviceBehavior The primary purpose of the PG pin is to indicate if the output voltage is in regulation, but it also indicates if the device is in thermal shutdown or disabled. Table 9-7 summarizes the behavior of the PG pin in a standalone or primary device. | Figure 9-5. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | <table><caption> Device Information</caption> <tr><td colspan=3 rowspan=3 >Part Number Current Rating Package TPS62870-Q1 6A TPS62871-Q1 9A RXS (VQFN-FCRLF, TPS62872-Q1 12 A 16) TPS62873-Q1 15 A</td></tr> </table> | Sheet名称：0_Instruction Manual; 内容摘要：使用说明; 项目适配说明：nan ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：1_Face; 内容摘要：文档封面页; 项目适配说明：需要根据实际项目更新编号， 明确并修改xxx的型号 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：2_Sign; 内容摘要：本文档的当前状态、编制、审核和批准人员的信息; 项目适配说明：需要根据具体项目，更新文档 的当前状态，编制、审核和批准人员的信息等 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：3_Overview; 内容摘要：说明本文档的目的，适用对象和应用范围; 项目适配说明：无须确认和更新，仅用于了解 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：4_Change History1; 内容摘要：本文档模板的变更履历; 项目适配说明：填写本文档模板的变更内容 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：5_Change History2; 内容摘要：结合实际项目，安全机制的变更履历; 项目适配说明：填写本文档安全机制的变更内容 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：6_Reference Document; 内容摘要：导出安全机制的参考文档; 项目适配说明：根据实际项目，需参考最新的 数据手册和安全手册 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：7_Definition&Arch; 内容摘要：xxx系列产品的定义及架构; 项目适配说明：需要需求开发人员在开发需求之前了解xxx系列产品的定义及架构 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：8_SG&FSR; 内容摘要：xxx系列产品的假设 安全目标和FSR; 项目适配说明：需要需求开发人员在开发需求 前了解xxx系列产品的安全目标和FSR ——0_Instruction Manual | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F13</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电。VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电。VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。 VCCIV8 is used to power EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH.VCC1V1 is used to power EQ6,DDR. For each Functional Safety-Capable current rating, there are full-featured device variants Documentation available to aid functional safety with I²C interface and limited-featured device variants system design without I2C interface. All devices offer high efficiency Documentation available to aid functional safety and ease of use. Low-resistance power switches system design allow up to 15-A continuous output current at high Documentation available to aid functional safety ambient temperatures. system design 2.7-V to 6-V input voltage range The devices can operate in stacked mode to deliver Family of pin-to-pin compatible devices: 6-A, 9-A, higher output currents or to spread the power 12-A, and 15-A dissipation across multiple devices. Four output voltage ranges: The TPS6287x-Q1 family implements an enhanced 0.4-V to 0.71875-V in 1.25-mV steps DCS control scheme that supports fast transients with 0.4-V to 1.0375-V in 2.5-mV steps fixed-frequency operation. Devices can operatein 0.4-V to 1.675-V in 5-mV steps power save mode for maximum efficiency, or forced- 0.8-V to 3.35-V in 10-mV steps PWM mode for the best transient performance and Output voltage accuracy ±1% lowest output voltage ripple. 7-mQ and 4.5-mQ internal power MOSFETs Adjustable external compensation An optional remote sensing feature maximizes voltage Resistor-selectable start-up output voltage and the device regulation at the point-of-load, Resistor-selectable switching frequency achieves better than ±1% DC voltage accuracy under Power-save or forced-PwM operation | None：U20; Two TPS6287x-Q1 Devices in a Stacked Configuration Figure 9-14 shows the recommended interconnections in a stack of two TPS6287x-Q1 devices. Primary Device CLOAD 100 nH VIN VIN SW 2.7Vto6V VIN MODE/SYNC Vio REr Load VOSNS EN GOSNS SDA 2 SCL RcoM VSEL FSEL 10pF CcOMP REsEL >RySEI COMP PG SYNC_OUT GND GND Secondary Device 100 nH VIN SW 333 VIN CIN MODE/SYNC VOSNS EN GOSNS SDA SCL VSEL VIc 10 pF FSEL COMP kQ PG PG SYNC_OUT GND GND 47kO | 9 Detailed Description 9.1overview The TPS6287x-Q1 devices are synchronous step-down (buck) DC/DC converters. These devices use an enhanced DCS control topology to achieve fast transient response while switching with a fixed frequency. Together, with their low output voltage ripple, high DC accuracy, and differential remote sensing, these devices are ideal for supplying the cores of modern high-performance processors. The family of devices includes 6-A, 9-A, 12-A, and 15-A devices. To further increase the output current capability, the user can combine multiple devices in a “stack". For example, a stack of two TPS62873-Q1 devices have a current capability of 30 A. Each device of a stack must have the same current rating to avoid that one device enters current limit too early. For each current rating, there are full-featured devices with an I2C interface and limited-featured devices without an I2C interface (see the Device Options). The user can use a device variant without I2C in exactly the same way as a device variant with I?C, except that: · The user must connect the unused SCL and SDA pins to GND · The user must be aware of the (fixed) factory settings for parameters and functions that are programmable in the I²C device variants. 9.2 Functional Block Diagram 9.3FeatureDescription 9.3.1 Fixed-Frequency DCS Control Topology TPS6287x-Q1 devices. This topology is comprised of an inner emulated current loop, a middle direct feedback loop, and an outer voltage-regulating loop. 9.3.2 Forced PwM and Power Save Modes The device can control the inductor current in three different ways to regulate the output: Pulse-width modulation with continuous inductor current (PWM-CCM) Pulse-width modulation with discontinuous inductor current (PWM-DCM) Pulse-frequency modulation with discontinuous inductor current and pulse skipping (PFM-DCM) During PWM-CCM operation, the device switches at a constant frequency and the inductor current is continuous (see Figure 9-2). PwM operation achieves the lowest output voltage ripple and the best transient performance. During PWM-DCM operation, the device switches at a constant frequency and the inductor current is discontinuous (see Figure 9-3). In this mode, the device controls the peak inductor current to maintain the selected switching frequency while still being able to regulate the output. | OTHERQUALIFIEDVERSIONSOFTPS62872-Q1,TPS62873-Q1: ·Catalog :TPS62872,TPS62873 NOTE: Qualified Version Definitions: ·Catalog - Tl's standard catalog product TAPEANDREELINFORMATION PACKAGE MATERIALSINFORMATION PLASTIC QUAD FLAT PACK- NO LEAD NOTES: All linear dimensions are in millimeters. TPS22918 实例清单 根据电路图证据[5][6][7]和文档证据[13][14][15][16][17][18][19]，共找到3个TPS22918实例，其参考编号为：**U1500**、**U1802**、**U1803**。 ### 2. 逐颗实例的输入、输出与ON控制 #### 实例1: U1500 * **输入电源 (VIN):** * **连接网络/电压:** VCC3V3 * **来源说明:** 文档证据[13]（HW Interface Component F16）和[18]（模块方案F16）中明确说明，该负载开关将“VCC3V3”电源转换为“VCC3V3_EQ”。此外，文档证据[16]（模块功能清单F16）也确认输入为“VCC3V3电压”。 * **输出电源 (VOUT):** * **连接网络/电压:** VCC3V3_EQ * **来源说明:** 证据[13]、[18]和[16]均指出输出为“VCC3V3_EQ”，用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电。 * **ON 控制:** * **连接网络/控制信号:** MCU_3V3_EQ_EN * **来源说明:** 证据[13]（HW Interface Component F16）明确指出，MCU通过引脚P20.3输出信号“MCU_3V3_EQ_EN”来控制该电源的使能。证据[17]（模块方案描述）也确认了“用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片”。 #### 实例2: U1802 * **输入电源 (VIN):** * **连接网络/电压:** VCC3V3 * **来源说明:** 文档证据[13]（HW Interface Component F17）和[19]（模块方案F17）中明确说明，该负载开关将“VCC3V3”电源转换为“VCC3V3_ETH”。证据[16]（模块功能清单F17）也确认输入为“VCC3V3输入”。 * **输出电源 (VOUT):** * **连接网络/电压:** VCC3V3_ETH * **来源说明:** 证据[13]、[19]和[16]均指出输出为“VCC3V3_ETH”，用于ETH供电。 * **ON 控制:** * **连接网络/控制信号:** MCU_3V3_ETH_EN * **来源说明:** 证据[13]（HW Interface Component F17）明确指出，MCU通过引脚P21.0输出信号“MCU_3V3_ETH_EN”来控制该电源的使能。证据[17]（模块方案描述）和[19]也确认了“用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片”。 #### 实例3: U1803 * **输入电源 (VIN):** * **连接网络/电压:** VCC1V8 * **来源说明:** 文档证据[14]（HW Interface Component，提及“使能TPS22918的1V8_EQ输出”）和[15]（模块方案F19）中说明，该负载开关将“VCC1V8电源”转换为“VCC1V8_EQ”。证据[16]（模块功能清单F19）确认输入为“VCC1V8电压”。 * **输出电源 (VOUT):** * **连接网络/电压:** VCC1V8_EQ * **来源说明:** 证据[14]、[15]和[16]均指出输出为“VCC1V8_EQ”，用于EQ6供电。 * **ON 控制:** * **连接网络/控制信号:** MCU_1V8_EQ_EN * **来源说明:** 证据[14]（HW Interface Component）指出，MCU通过引脚P20.0输出信号“MCU_1V8_EQ_EN”来控制该电源的使能。证据[15]（模块方案F19）和[17]（模块方案描述）也确认了“用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片”。 ### 3. 缺失信息 * **电路图直接连接：** 提供的电路图证据[5][6][7]仅确认了三个元件的型号和位置，未直接显示各个引脚（VIN, VOUT, ON）所连接的具体网络名称。上述答案中的网络名称（如VCC3V3, VCC3V3_EQ, MCU_3V3_EQ_EN等）均来自文档证据，而非电路图。电路图证据中未包含这些连接细节。 ### 4. 证据冲突 在提供的证据覆盖度矩阵中，存在一些冲突，但主要与名为“600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx”的文档中对某些字段的取值不一致有关，例如“hw interface component”字段同时有“b”和“qm”值。**这些冲突不涉及TPS22918的输入、输出或ON控制信号的具体网络名称**，因此不影响上述答案的准确性。

Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F16</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关TPS22918进行设计。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3-EQ is used for the IO and camera power supply of EQ6, and the load switch TPS22918 is used for the design.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>采用TPS22918负载开关，将VCC3V3系统电源转换为VCC3V3_EQ，提供给EQ6和摄像头供电。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 The TPS22918 load switch is used to convert the VCC3V3 system power to VCC3V3-EQ to supply power to EQ6 and the camera. hw-v1-circuit-vcc3v3-rolesuccess0.4900.4611.000not_applicable0.5000.5001.0000.351not_applicable
问题：VCC3V3 在板上主要给哪些电源器件供电？不要把电阻电容当成主要负载。
参考答案：VCC3V3 是主要 3.3V 电源轨，供给 U1700、U1800、U1500、U1802、U1804 和 U1600 等 IC；回答应聚焦芯片而非无源器件。
实际回答：根据检索到的证据，VCC3V3 在板上主要给以下电源器件供电（已排除电阻和电容）： ### 直接由 VCC3V3 供电的电源器件（位号和类型） * **U1500**: 电源管理芯片（DC-DC），其 VIN 引脚连接 VCC3V3，用于生成 VCC3V3_ETH 等电源轨。 * **来源**: 证据[2] (电路图) * **U1600**: 电源芯片，其 VCCIN 引脚连接 VCC3V3。 * **来源**: 证据[2] (电路图) * **U1700**: 电源芯片，其 VIN_B0_1, VIN_B1_1, VANA, VIN_B0_2, VIN_B1_2 等多个引脚连接 VCC3V3。 * **来源**: 证据[2] (电路图) * **U1800**: 电源芯片，其 VIN 和 MODE/SYNC 引脚连接 VCC3V3。 * **来源**: 证据[2] (电路图) * **U1802**: 电源芯片，其 VIN 引脚连接 VCC3V3。 * **来源**: 证据[2] (电路图) * **U1804**: 电源芯片，其 VIN 引脚连接 VCC3V3。 * **来源**: 证据[2] (电路图) * **Q1700**: MOSFET 功率开关管，其源极(S)连接 VCC3V3，用于生成 VCC3V3_SW 网络。 * **来源**: 证据[2] (电路图) * **X2000**: 振荡器/晶振，其电源引脚连接 VCC3V3。 * **来源**: 证据[2] (电路图) * **L1601, L1701**: 电感，一端连接 VCC3V3，作为电源转换的一部分。 * **来源**: 证据[2] (电路图) ### 由 VCC3V3 衍生出的子电源网络供电的器件 VCC3V3 通过上述电源器件（如 U1500, U1802, Q1700 等）转换为多个子电源网络，这些网络再为其他主要器件供电： * **VCC3V3_MCU**: 给 **MCU (U900C)** 的多个电源域（如 VEXT, VFLEX, VDDM, VAREF1, VEVRSB, VDDP3）供电，同时也给 **U1200, U1203, U1204, U1205, U1302, U1303, U1304, U1305, U503** 等芯片的 VI/O 或 VCCB 引脚供电，以及给 **DP907** 供电。 * **来源**: 证据[7] (电路图), 证据[10], [11], [13], [15] (文档) * **VCC3V3_EQ**: 给 **U1802** 的 VOUT 引脚提供输出，并给 **U400-2** 的多个 VDD_RING 引脚供电，以及给 **Y500** 供电。 * **来源**: 证据[5] (电路图) * **VCC3V3_ETH**: 给 **U1500** 的 VOUT 和 QOD 引脚提供输出，并给 **U1501** 的 EN 和 IN 引脚供电。 * **来源**: 证据[6] (电路图) * **VCC3V3_SW**: 给 **Q1700** 的漏极(D)和 **U901** 的 S2 引脚供电。 * **来源**: 证据[8] (电路图) * **VCC3V3_CAM_ACQ**: 给 **U900A** 的 ADC 输入引脚供电。 * **来源**: 证据[3] (电路图) * **VCC0V75**: 由 VCC3V3 通过 TPS62872 转换，用于 **EQ6** 的内核供电。 * **来源**: 证据[9], [16] (文档) * **0V75_MIPI_DDR**: 由 VCC3V3 通过 SA23002 转换，用于 **EQ6** 的 IO 口供电。 * **来源**: 证据[9], [14], [16] (文档) * **VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC50**: 由 VCC3V3 通过 LP87702 PMIC 转换，分别用于 **EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH** 以及 **CAN收发器** 供电。 * **来源**: 证据[9], [16] (文档) * **VCC1V25_MCU**: 由 VCC3V3_MCU 通过 MCU 内置控制器与外部MOS/电感/电容构成的 BUCK 电源转换，用于 **MCU** 的核供电。 * **来源**: 证据[9], [15] (文档) ### 证据冲突说明 检索到的证据中存在一些冲突，主要涉及 PDF 文档中的表格格式和寄存器描述，但这些冲突不影响上述关于 VCC3V3 供电器件的结论。具体冲突如下： 1. **字段“td colspan”**: 在 PDF 文档 `FF4D6750FC7339E769C3F6246C6F971C.pdf` 中，表格的 `td colspan` 属性存在不一致的值（`2`, `3`, `4`），这仅影响表格渲染，不涉及电气连接。 2. **字段“operation disabled 1b”**: 在 `CONTROL1` 寄存器描述中，关于“SSC operation disabled”的描述，一处写为“hiccup”，另一处写为“ssc”，这可能是文档中的笔误或描述不清，但寄存器功能定义是明确的。 3. **字段“ob”**: 在 `CONTROL1` 寄存器描述中，`PG` 引脚的功能描述中，一处写为“en”，另一处写为“pg”，这可能是文档格式问题，引脚功能是明确的。 ### 缺失信息 当前证据已完整覆盖问题，未发现缺失信息。
评分正文：根据检索到的证据，VCC3V3 在板上主要给以下电源器件供电（已排除电阻和电容）： ### 直接由 VCC3V3 供电的电源器件（位号和类型） * **U1500**: 电源管理芯片（DC-DC），其 VIN 引脚连接 VCC3V3，用于生成 VCC3V3_ETH 等电源轨。 * **来源**: 证据[2] (电路图) * **U1600**: 电源芯片，其 VCCIN 引脚连接 VCC3V3。 * **来源**: 证据[2] (电路图) * **U1700**: 电源芯片，其 VIN_B0_1, VIN_B1_1, VANA, VIN_B0_2, VIN_B1_2 等多个引脚连接 VCC3V3。 * **来源**: 证据[2] (电路图) * **U1800**: 电源芯片，其 VIN 和 MODE/SYNC 引脚连接 VCC3V3。 * **来源**: 证据[2] (电路图) * **U1802**: 电源芯片，其 VIN 引脚连接 VCC3V3。 * **来源**: 证据[2] (电路图) * **U1804**: 电源芯片，其 VIN 引脚连接 VCC3V3。 * **来源**: 证据[2] (电路图) * **Q1700**: MOSFET 功率开关管，其源极(S)连接 VCC3V3，用于生成 VCC3V3_SW 网络。 * **来源**: 证据[2] (电路图) * **X2000**: 振荡器/晶振，其电源引脚连接 VCC3V3。 * **来源**: 证据[2] (电路图) * **L1601, L1701**: 电感，一端连接 VCC3V3，作为电源转换的一部分。 * **来源**: 证据[2] (电路图) ### 由 VCC3V3 衍生出的子电源网络供电的器件 VCC3V3 通过上述电源器件（如 U1500, U1802, Q1700 等）转换为多个子电源网络，这些网络再为其他主要器件供电： * **VCC3V3_MCU**: 给 **MCU (U900C)** 的多个电源域（如 VEXT, VFLEX, VDDM, VAREF1, VEVRSB, VDDP3）供电，同时也给 **U1200, U1203, U1204, U1205, U1302, U1303, U1304, U1305, U503** 等芯片的 VI/O 或 VCCB 引脚供电，以及给 **DP907** 供电。 * **来源**: 证据[7] (电路图), 证据[10], [11], [13], [15] (文档) * **VCC3V3_EQ**: 给 **U1802** 的 VOUT 引脚提供输出，并给 **U400-2** 的多个 VDD_RING 引脚供电，以及给 **Y500** 供电。 * **来源**: 证据[5] (电路图) * **VCC3V3_ETH**: 给 **U1500** 的 VOUT 和 QOD 引脚提供输出，并给 **U1501** 的 EN 和 IN 引脚供电。 * **来源**: 证据[6] (电路图) * **VCC3V3_SW**: 给 **Q1700** 的漏极(D)和 **U901** 的 S2 引脚供电。 * **来源**: 证据[8] (电路图) * **VCC3V3_CAM_ACQ**: 给 **U900A** 的 ADC 输入引脚供电。 * **来源**: 证据[3] (电路图) * **VCC0V75**: 由 VCC3V3 通过 TPS62872 转换，用于 **EQ6** 的内核供电。 * **来源**: 证据[9], [16] (文档) * **0V75_MIPI_DDR**: 由 VCC3V3 通过 SA23002 转换，用于 **EQ6** 的 IO 口供电。 * **来源**: 证据[9], [14], [16] (文档) * **VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC50**: 由 VCC3V3 通过 LP87702 PMIC 转换，分别用于 **EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH** 以及 **CAN收发器** 供电。 * **来源**: 证据[9], [16] (文档) * **VCC1V25_MCU**: 由 VCC3V3_MCU 通过 MCU 内置控制器与外部MOS/电感/电容构成的 BUCK 电源转换，用于 **MCU** 的核供电。 * **来源**: 证据[9], [15] (文档) ### 证据冲突说明 检索到的证据中存在一些冲突，主要涉及 PDF 文档中的表格格式和寄存器描述，但这些冲突不影响上述关于 VCC3V3 供电器件的结论。具体冲突如下： 1. **字段“td colspan”**: 在 PDF 文档 `FF4D6750FC7339E769C3F6246C6F971C.pdf` 中，表格的 `td colspan` 属性存在不一致的值（`2`, `3`, `4`），这仅影响表格渲染，不涉及电气连接。 2. **字段“operation disabled 1b”**: 在 `CONTROL1` 寄存器描述中，关于“SSC operation disabled”的描述，一处写为“hiccup”，另一处写为“ssc”，这可能是文档中的笔误或描述不清，但寄存器功能定义是明确的。 3. **字段“ob”**: 在 `CONTROL1` 寄存器描述中，`PG` 引脚的功能描述中，一处写为“en”，另一处写为“pg”，这可能是文档格式问题，引脚功能是明确的。 ### 缺失信息 当前证据已完整覆盖问题，未发现缺失信息。

Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . FPWM Mode and Power-Save Mode Selection Note</caption> <tr><td >MODE/SYNCPin</td><td >FPWMEN Bit</td><td >OperatingMode</td><td >Remark</td></tr> <tr><td >Low</td><td >0</td><td >PSM</td><td >Do not use in a stacked configuration.</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td >1</td><td >FPWM</td><td></td></tr> <tr><td >High</td><td >X</td><td >FPWM</td><td></td></tr> <tr><td >Sync Clock</td><td >X</td><td >FPWM</td><td></td></tr> </table> | In standalone (nonstacked) applications, the user can disable the active pulldown of the EN pin if the user sets SINGLE = 1 in the CONTROL3 register. Fault conditions have no effect on the EN pin when SINGLE = 1 (the EN pin is always pulled down during device initialization). In stacked applications, ensure that SINGLE = 0. When the internal SYSTEM_READY signal is low (that is, initialization is complete and there are no fault conditions), the internal open-drain transistor is high impedance and the EN pin functions like a standard input. A high level on the EN pin enables the DC/DC converter in the device. A low level disables the DC/DC converter (the I2C interface is enabled as soon as the device has completed its initialization and is not affected by the state of the internal ENABLE or SYSTEM_READY signals). A low level on the EN pin forces the device into shutdown. During shutdown, the MOSFETs in the power stage are off, the internal control circuitry is disabled, and the device consumes only 20 μA (typical). The rising threshold voltage of the EN pin is 1.0 V and the falling threshold voltage is 0.9 V. The tolerance of the threshold voltages is ±30 mV, which means that the user can use the EN pin to implement precise turn-on and turn-off behavior. When power is applied to the VIN pin, the toggling of the EN pin does not reset the loaded default register settings. 9.3.4 Start-Up When the voltage on the VIN pin exceeds the positive-going UVLO threshold, the device initializes as follows: ·The device pulls the EN pin low. ·The device enables the internal reference voltage. · The device reads the state of the VSEL, FSEL, and SYNC_OUT pins. · The device loads the default values into the device registers. When initialization is complete, the device enables I²C communication and releases the EN pin. The external circuitry controlling the EN pin now determines the behavior of the device: · If the EN pin is low, the device is disabled. The user can write to and read from the device registers, but the DC/DC converter does not operate · If the EN pin is high, the device is enabled. The user can write to and read from the device registers and, after a short delay, the DC/DC converter starts to ramp up its output. The SSTIME[1:0] bits in the CONTROL2 register select the duration of the soft-start ramp: | <table> <tr><td></td><td></td><td></td><td >MIN</td><td >NOM</td><td >MAX</td><td >UNIT</td></tr> <tr><td >VIN</td><td >Input voltage</td><td >VIN SDA, SCL</td><td >2.7</td><td></td><td >6 5</td><td >V</td></tr> <tr><td >VouT</td><td >Output voltage</td><td></td><td >0.4</td><td></td><td >3.35 V or (ViN - 1.4</td><td></td></tr> <tr><td></td><td></td><td></td><td></td><td></td><td >V)(1)</td><td></td></tr> <tr><td></td><td></td><td >TPS62870-Q1</td><td></td><td></td><td >6</td><td></td></tr> <tr><td></td><td></td><td >TPS62871-Q1</td><td></td><td></td><td >9</td><td></td></tr> <tr><td >loUT</td><td >Output current</td><td >TPS62872-Q1</td><td></td><td></td><td >12</td><td >A</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td></td><td >TPS62873-Q1</td><td></td><td></td><td >15</td><td></td></tr> <tr><td></td><td >Inductance</td><td></td><td >110</td><td></td><td >330</td><td >nH</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td></td><td >fsw≥2.25MHz andVouT≤1.675V</td><td >55</td><td></td><td >330</td><td></td></tr> </table> | VOUT(VIN - VoUT IL(PP) = VU(NxLxW) | <table><caption> Table 9-16. 问题：软件配置 LP87702 应查 HSI 的哪一节？
参考答案：应查 HSI 7.2 节“LP87702 配置”，其中给出软件寄存器和 I²C 配置说明。
实际回答：根据现有证据，关于“软件配置 LP87702 应查 HSI 的哪一节”的问题，回答如下： **应查阅 HSI 文档的“7.2 LP87702配置 LP87702 Configuration”章节。** * **来源说明**：在 HSI 文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 的目录结构（证据[3]）中，明确列出了“7.2 LP87702配置 LP87702 Configuration”。该章节的内容（证据[2]）也进一步说明：“LP87702运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器如下所示：Table 5-2 LP87702 Configuration”。 **缺失信息**：无。该问题已完整覆盖。 **证据冲突**： 在检索到的证据中，存在以下冲突，但**不影响**上述主问题的答案： 1. **字段“none”**：在 HSI 文档的不同表格中，该字段被赋予了不同且不相关的值（如“1”、“a17”、“b”、“cfg1”、“flash”、“gpio”、“i2c0_scl”、“lp87702”、“mcu”、“p11.14”、“p15.4”、“qm”、“v1.0”等）。这些可能是表格中的占位符或不同行的数据。 2. **字段“td colspan”**：在 PDF 文档中，表格的 `td colspan` 属性存在不同值（2、3、4），这属于文档格式差异。 3. **字段“operation disabled 1b”**：在描述 TPS62872 芯片 CONTROL1 寄存器的表格中，该字段的描述存在冲突，一处（证据[15]）显示为“hiccup”，另一处（证据[17]）显示为“ssc”。**注意**：此冲突与 LP87702 芯片的配置无关，而是关于另一个芯片 TPS62872 的寄存器描述。

READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. ACTIVE The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The regulated DC/DC converter is in the enabled state. All the registers can be read and written by the MCU through the system serial port. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td>1、VANAUVLO阈值电压 2、NRST阈值电压 </td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | None：U20; 问题：网表写 TC367、接口文档写 TC377，这两个说法怎么处理？
参考答案：必须保留来源差异：EDF 的 U900 型号是 SAK-TC367DP-64F300S，而 HSI 章节名是 TC377 最小系统；现有证据不足以把二者无条件视为同一准确型号。
实际回答：根据您提供的证据，网表与接口文档中芯片型号不一致的问题处理如下： ### 1. 网表中是如何描述TC367的？ * **证据来源**：电路设计文件 `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`。 * **描述**：网表中存在多个以“TC367”命名的网络，例如： * `TC367_JTAG_TCK/DAP0` * `TC367_JTAG_TDI` * `TC367_JTAG_TDO/DAP2` * `TC367_JTAG_TMS/DAP1` * `TC367_JTAG_TRST` * `TC367_RESET_N` * **结论**：网表的网络命名明确指向了“TC367”这一型号。 ### 2. 接口文档中是如何描述TC377的？ * **证据来源**：文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`。 * **描述**：接口文档中明确将主控MCU芯片定义为“TC377”。具体包括： * 文档目录和章节标题中包含“TC377最小系统”。 * 硬件模块表格中描述：“32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有**SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA**主控芯片”。 * 模块功能定义表格中，将MCU的功能安全需求列为B(B)，并关联到型号“SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA”。 * **结论**：接口文档的硬件描述和模块定义明确指向了“SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA”（即TC377）这一型号。 ### 3. TC367和TC377是否是同一芯片的不同版本或别名？ * **证据来源**：对比上述两个来源的证据。 * **分析**：目前提供的证据中，**没有任何直接说明**TC367和TC377是同一芯片的不同版本或存在官方替代关系。网表仅使用“TC367”作为网络命名前缀，而接口文档中定义的主控芯片型号是 `SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA`。文档中没有提及 `TC367` 这个型号。 * **结论**：**现有证据无法确认TC367和TC377是同一芯片**。这很可能是一个设计错误或版本不一致问题。 ### 4. 针对这种不一致，通常的处理流程或建议是什么？ * **证据来源**：文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 中的模板使用说明。 * **建议**： 1. **设计确认**：需要由硬件设计工程师确认，正确的、实际使用的芯片型号是 `SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA`（接口文档中的型号），还是`SAK-TC367...`（网表命名所暗示的型号）。 2. **文档对齐**：一旦确认了正确的型号，需要统一更新所有相关文档。根据模板说明，当内容不涉及特定要求时，可进行裁剪；但核心信息（如芯片型号）必须保持一致。 3. **变更管理**：如果确认是芯片型号发生了变更（例如从TC377替换为TC367），应遵循项目变更管理流程，更新网表、接口文档以及其他相关设计文件，并记录变更历史。 ### 缺失信息 * 目前缺少以下关键信息，导致无法做出最终判断： * **芯片的数据手册(DataSheet)**：没有提供任何关于TC367或TC377的具体数据手册，无法从官方渠道确认两者是否为同一芯片的不同版本或替代关系。 * **电子表格或其他官方对照表**：没有提供用于确认型号替代关系的电子表格或官方文档。 ### 证据冲突说明 根据提供的证据冲突列表，虽然存在一些字段冲突（如 `id`, `sm name`, `accuracy` 等），但这些冲突与当前问题“网表写TC367、接口文档写TC377”**没有直接关联**。当前问题的核心冲突是：网表（电路设计）和接口文档（系统描述）对同一模块（MCU最小系统）所使用的芯片型号描述不一致。
评分正文：根据您提供的证据，网表与接口文档中芯片型号不一致的问题处理如下： ### 1. 网表中是如何描述TC367的？ * **证据来源**：电路设计文件 `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`。 * **描述**：网表中存在多个以“TC367”命名的网络，例如： * `TC367_JTAG_TCK/DAP0` * `TC367_JTAG_TDI` * `TC367_JTAG_TDO/DAP2` * `TC367_JTAG_TMS/DAP1` * `TC367_JTAG_TRST` * `TC367_RESET_N` * **结论**：网表的网络命名明确指向了“TC367”这一型号。 ### 2. 接口文档中是如何描述TC377的？ * **证据来源**：文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`。 * **描述**：接口文档中明确将主控MCU芯片定义为“TC377”。具体包括： * 文档目录和章节标题中包含“TC377最小系统”。 * 硬件模块表格中描述：“32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有**SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA**主控芯片”。 * 模块功能定义表格中，将MCU的功能安全需求列为B(B)，并关联到型号“SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA”。 * **结论**：接口文档的硬件描述和模块定义明确指向了“SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA”（即TC377）这一型号。 ### 3. TC367和TC377是否是同一芯片的不同版本或别名？ * **证据来源**：对比上述两个来源的证据。 * **分析**：目前提供的证据中，**没有任何直接说明**TC367和TC377是同一芯片的不同版本或存在官方替代关系。网表仅使用“TC367”作为网络命名前缀，而接口文档中定义的主控芯片型号是 `SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA`。文档中没有提及 `TC367` 这个型号。 * **结论**：**现有证据无法确认TC367和TC377是同一芯片**。这很可能是一个设计错误或版本不一致问题。 ### 4. 针对这种不一致，通常的处理流程或建议是什么？ * **证据来源**：文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 中的模板使用说明。 * **建议**： 1. **设计确认**：需要由硬件设计工程师确认，正确的、实际使用的芯片型号是 `SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA`（接口文档中的型号），还是`SAK-TC367...`（网表命名所暗示的型号）。 2. **文档对齐**：一旦确认了正确的型号，需要统一更新所有相关文档。根据模板说明，当内容不涉及特定要求时，可进行裁剪；但核心信息（如芯片型号）必须保持一致。 3. **变更管理**：如果确认是芯片型号发生了变更（例如从TC377替换为TC367），应遵循项目变更管理流程，更新网表、接口文档以及其他相关设计文件，并记录变更历史。 ### 缺失信息 * 目前缺少以下关键信息，导致无法做出最终判断： * **芯片的数据手册(DataSheet)**：没有提供任何关于TC367或TC377的具体数据手册，无法从官方渠道确认两者是否为同一芯片的不同版本或替代关系。 * **电子表格或其他官方对照表**：没有提供用于确认型号替代关系的电子表格或官方文档。 ### 证据冲突说明 根据提供的证据冲突列表，虽然存在一些字段冲突（如 `id`, `sm name`, `accuracy` 等），但这些冲突与当前问题“网表写TC367、接口文档写TC377”**没有直接关联**。当前问题的核心冲突是：网表（电路设计）和接口文档（系统描述）对同一模块（MCU最小系统）所使用的芯片型号描述不一致。

The overall hardware architecture is described as follows:整体系统由摄像头板（CAM）和主板（MB）两部分组成。The overall system is composed of two parts: camera board (CAM) and mainboard (MB).前向摄像头采集道路图像后通过数字接口输出视频数据。The forward camera collects road images and outputs video data through the digital interface.数字视频数据通过线缆传输到主板上的视频处理器EYEQ6进行视频处理。The digital video data is transmitted to the video processor EYEQ6 on the mainboard through cables for video processing.EYEQ6处理视频数据，输出路况信息。EYEQ6 processes video data and outputs road condition information.路况信息通过SPI口传送给MCU进行处理，主板通过CAN对外传送路况信息。Road condition information is transmitted to MCU through SPI interface for processing, and the mainboard transmits road condition information externally through CAN.处理结果通过CAN发出，包括对车的主动安全控制信号以及给驾驶员的报警信号。The processing result is sent out through CAN, including the active safety control signal for the car and the alarm signal for the driver.图 1 系统工作方式Fig 1 System operating block硬件框图 Hardware block diagram图 2 系统框图Fig 2 system Any usage of a Mobileye safety-critical technology in applications with autonomous level higher than L2 (hands-free) shall be discussed and approved by Mobileye. ——SYS SM ID：EyeQ4L_AouReq_2308; SM Name：FuSa-2308-Final Braking Profiles; SM TSR：System level assumption: The SYS shall send the final braking profiles file to Mobileye. ——SYS SM ID：EyeQ4L_AouReq_2422; SM Name：FuSa-2422-Suppression of Safety-Critical Signals; SM TSR：EyeQ4L failsafe文档描述，假设相关输入是valid。 Mobileye assums that the relevant input signals (ego-speed, yaw rate, etc) are valid. If not, the failsafe algorithms still run, but without taking into account the input-signal limits. As a result, the failsafes module behavior is unpredictable. FuSa-2422 描述：如果使能了failsafe功能，那么相关的信号可以认为是QM的？ Fusa 2336描述，MCU 不要对信号的invalidity进行filter 可能性A: 如果使能了 EyeQ4L的failsafe，那么MCU需要保证输入信号的有效性。 有效时，信号的转发功能可以是QM的；输入信号无效时，不相信EQ的判断 B： 不使用EyeQ4L的 failsafe功能，MCU 信号转发是 ASILB的，MCU不进行信号有效性过滤？？？然后呢，EyeQ4L对于无效的信号有什么策略？ ——SYS SM ID：EyeQ4L_AouReq_2423; SM Name：FuSa-2423-Braking Controller Monitors Mobileye AEB Decisions; SM TSR：Software level assumption: It is assumed that the vehicle braking controller samples Mobileye AEB decisions and follows them throughout the braking operation. Signal existence and non-existence is expected to be followed by braking and stopping braking respectively. ——SYS SM | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片和晶体、硬件配置组成，实现SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA最小硬件系统工作单元，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 32-bit MCU chip TC377 minimum system module has SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA main control chip and crystal, hardware configuration, to achieve SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA minimum hardware system work unit, functional safety level up to ASIL-D, meet the application requirements. </td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>处理器 根据ADAS产品需求，综合评估选型为32位MCU芯片SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA， MCU接口资源丰富，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 Processor According to ADAS product requirements, 32-bit MCU chip SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA is selected through comprehensive evaluation, which has rich MCU interface resources and the highest functional safety level can reach ASIL-D to meet application requirements 时钟设计 硬件选型20MHz晶振给MCU提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。 Clock design Hardware selection of clock design 20MHz crystal oscillator provides clock input to MCU, and its internal PLL and other circuits complete frequency doubling and frequency division for its kernel and each module. 复位及watchdog 系统复位及看门狗芯片采用LP87702内部看门狗，喂狗方式为窗口喂狗，当喂狗窗口无有效喂狗次数超过OTP配置时，将触发整个系统复位。 a) 上电时，看门狗芯片对MCU启动过程进行监控，同时对MCU的电源VCC3V3电压进行低压监控，异常时主动复位MCU。 b) 运行过程中由看门狗监控MCU运行状态，当MCU程序跑飞时看门狗超时触发复位信号主动复位MCU。 Reset and watchdog The system reset and watchdog chip adopts LP87702 internal watchdog, and the feeding method is window feeding. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. Power demand The MCU needs 1 channel VCC3V3_MCU (accuracy: 3.3V±10%) and 1 channel VCC1V25_MCU (accuracy: 1.2-1.35V) power supply.</td></tr></table> | Unnamed: 0：nan; Unnamed: 1：nan; Unnamed: 2：nan; NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | No.：5; Alarm ID：ALMx[5]; Alarm name：(x=0..5) Miscellaneous error detection; Related SMs：SM[HW]:CPU.PTAG:SM_CONTROL; Safety related ?：Y; Fault Handling (Refer to Note)：Internal actions: • INTERRUPT REQUEST or NMI TRAP REQUEST • 中断触发后，软件在中断中保存SMU_AG到一片外狗复位不清除的RAM区，然后通过外狗进行复位 • 软件在TRAP中，先判断SMU_AG是否为0。若SMU_AG≠0，则保存SMU_AG到一片外狗复位不清除的RAM区，然后通过外狗进行复位。若SMU_AG=0，则不保存SMU_AG。; Status：nan; Person in charge：nan; Comment：cpu0和cpu1 ——12.1 TC37X_HWSM No.：6; Alarm ID：ALMx[6]; Alarm name：(x=0..5) Single bit error correction; Related SMs：SM[HW]:CPU.PCACHE:ERROR_CORRECTION SM[HW]:CPU.PSPR:ERROR_CORRECTION SM[HW]:VMT:MBIST; Safety related ?：N; Fault Handling (Refer to Note)：• NO ACTION; Status：nan; Person in charge：nan; Comment：cpu0和cpu1 ——12.1 TC37X_HWSM No.：7; Alarm ID：ALMx[7]; Alarm name：(x=0..5) Uncorrectable critical error detection; Related SMs：SM[HW]:CPU.PCACHE:ADDRESS SM[HW]:CPU.PCACHE:CONTROL SM[HW]:CPU.PCACHE:ERROR_CORRECTION SM[HW]:CPU.PCACHE:ERROR_MANAGEMENT SM[HW]:CPU.PCACHE:REG_MONITOR SM[HW]:CPU.PSPR:ADDRESS SM[HW]:CPU.PSPR:CONTROL SM[HW]:CPU.PSPR:ERROR_CORRECTION SM[HW]:CPU.PSPR:ERROR_MANAGEMENT SM[HW]:CPU.PSPR:REG_MONITOR SM[HW]:VMT:MBIST; Safety related ?：Y; Fault Handling (Refer to Note)：Internal actions: • INTERRUPT REQUEST or NMI TRAP REQUEST • 中断触发后，软件在中断中保存SMU_AG到一片外狗复位不清除的RAM区，然后通过外狗进行复位 • 软件在TRAP中，先判断SMU_AG是否为0。若SMU_AG≠0，则保存SMU_AG到一片外狗复位不清除的RAM区，然后通过外狗进行复位。若SMU_AG=0，则不保存SMU_AG。; Status：nan; Person in charge：nan; Comment：cpu0和cpu1 ——12.1 TC37X_HWSM | <table><caption> Device Information</caption> <tr><td colspan=3 rowspan=3 >Part Number Current Rating Package TPS62870-Q1 6A TPS62871-Q1 9A RXS (VQFN-FCRLF, TPS62872-Q1 12 A 16) TPS62873-Q1 15 A</td></tr> </table> | all operating conditions. 问题：忽略无关的摄像头和 CAN 内容，只结合电路与 HSI 说明 LP87702 的供电输出和配置。
参考答案：LP87702-Q1 是 U1700，输入 VCC3V3，关键输出为 VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50；配置依据 HSI 7.2 节，回答不应混入无关摄像头或 CAN 结论。
实际回答：好的，已为您忽略无关的摄像头和 CAN 内容，仅基于电路与 HSI 文档分析 LP87702 的供电输出和配置。 ### 基于电路与 HSI 文档的 LP87702 供电输出和配置 #### 子问题 1：LP87702 的供电输出引脚、电压、电流等参数 **答案：** 根据 HSI 文档，LP87702 是一款 PMIC（电源管理集成电路），其功能是将 VCC3V3 输入电压转换为三路输出： * **VCC1V8**: 用于 EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH 供电。 * **VCC1V1**: 用于 EQ6、DDR 供电。 * **VCC50**: 用于 CAN 收发器供电。 **来源说明：** * 证据[1]（文档 `b6943ae371d76077`）和证据[13]（文档 `5e69a562758e3536`）均明确描述了 LP87702 的输入电压为 VCC3V3，输出为 VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50，并说明了各自的用途。 * 证据[6]（文档 `44c4021cbbe6e903`）的功能定义表格中也确认了“LP87702，VCC3V3电压输入，VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50电压输出”。 **缺失信息：** * **输出电流：** 所有提供的证据中均未提及 VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50 这三路输出的具体电流规格（如最大输出电流或额定电流）。 * **具体引脚号：** 证据中未提及 LP87702 芯片的具体引脚编号，仅描述了其输出的电压网络名称。 --- #### 子问题 2：LP87702 的配置寄存器、模式设置、使能条件等配置信息 **答案：** 根据 HSI 文档，LP87702 的配置和使能信息如下： 1. **使能条件：** * **VCC50 使能：** MCU 通过其 GPIO 引脚 `P15.3`（信号名 `MCU_5V_EN`）控制 VCC50 的使能与关断。当该引脚为高电平时，使能 VCC50 输出；低电平时关断 VCC50 输出。 * **VCC1V8 和 VCC1V1 使能：** MCU 通过 I2C 总线（`MCU_SCL_P15.4`， `MCU_SDA_P15.5`）通信来设置 VCC1V8 和 VCC1V1 的输出电压并使能它们。 * **复位：** MCU 通过 GPIO 引脚 `P12.1` 复位该芯片。 2. **配置寄存器：** * HSI 文档明确指出，LP87702 运行期间需要 MCU 通过 I2C 配置寄存器。具体配置的寄存器内容在文档中通过表格形式给出（原文为 `Table 5-2 LP87702 Configuration`），但在此次检索的文本证据中，该表格的具体内容未被截取呈现。 3. **运行模式：** * LP87702 支持多种运行模式，包括：`SHUTDOWN`（关机）、`READ OTP`（读取 OTP）、`STANDBY`（待机）和 `ACTIVE`（激活）。 * **模式切换条件：** 模式的切换与电源电压 `VANA`、`NRST` 引脚电压及使能信号有关。例如，在 `ACTIVE` 模式下，稳压 DC/DC 变换器处于使能状态，所有寄存器均可通过系统串口（I2C）读写。 4. **功能安全等级：** 该模块的功能安全等级为 `QM(B)`。 **来源说明：** * 使能和控制信息来源于证据[1]（文档 `b6943ae371d76077`）、证据[13]（文档 `5e69a562758e3536`）和证据[16]（文档 `a6f318d1053a4c7e`）。 * 运行模式信息来源于证据[3]（文档 `ea7b2234534382bf`）。 * 配置寄存器信息来源于证据[9]（文档 `d69d04d302326d46`），该文档明确指出“LP87702运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器”。 * 功能安全等级信息来源于证据[6]（文档 `44c4021cbbe6e903`）。 **缺失信息：** * **具体寄存器配置值：** HSI 文档引用了配置表格（Table 5-2），但当前检索到的文本碎片中未包含该表格的具体内容。因此，无法提供具体的寄存器地址、需要写入的值等详细信息。 --- ### 证据冲突说明 根据检索到的证据覆盖度矩阵，存在以下冲突，这些冲突与 LP87702 的供电输出和配置问题**不直接相关**，但为遵守指令，特此列出： * **CAN 收发器芯片型号冲突：** 关于 `tcan1044a` 字段，不同证据给出了 `1` 和 `tcan1145` 两个取值。 * **MC 寄存器值冲突：** 关于 `mc` 字段，不同证据给出了 `001`、`100`、`111` 三个取值。 * **ID 冲突：** 关于 `id` 字段，不同证据给出了 `88`、`91`、`swrs_tc3xx_030`、`swrs_tc3xx_031`、`swrs_tc3xx_032`、`swrs_tc3xx_040` 等多个取值。 * **SM 名称冲突：** 关于 `sm name` 字段，不同证据给出了 `limitation`、`smc`、`watch` 三个取值。 * **SM TSR 冲突：** 关于 `sm tsr` 字段，不同证据给出了 `ecu`、`in`、`the` 三个取值。 * **无字段名冲突：** 关于 `none` 字段，不同证据给出了大量不一致的取值，如 `vcc50`、`p11.14`、`flash` 等。 * **负载值冲突：** 关于 `load` 字段，不同证据给出了 `0.75 ω`、`7.5`、`7.5 ω` 三个取值。 **注意：** 以上冲突主要来源于 HSI 文档中关于 CAN 收发器、安全机制、MCU 引脚配置等章节，与 LP87702 的电源输出和配置无关，系文档中不同部分的描述。

检索上下文：Instance U1700: LP87702-Q1, 20200424007, LP87702RRHBRQ1, pqfn50p5x5x90-32. | Instance X1903: FPC_30PIN+2NC, 101106-203050-HF, FPC_30PIN+2NC, CONN_101106-203050-HF-30PIN. | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F11/F12/F13/F14/F15/F16/F17/F18/F19/F25/F27</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. Among these voltages, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM have voltage ranges within the measurement range and are directly sampled. VCC3V3_EQ and VCC3V3_CAM are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_EQ is sampled using AN2, VCC1V8 using AN3, VCC1V1 using AN4, VCC1V8_EQ using AN5, VCC3V3_CAM using AN12, VCC1V1_CAM using AN13, VCC_1V8_CAM using AN14, 0V75_MIPI_DDR using AN18, and VCC0V75 using AN19. CAN&ETH电源电压采样 系统需求MCU对LP87702、TPS22918的输出电压VCC3V3_ETH，VCC50进行ADC采样和异常监控，VCC3V3_ETH和VCC50经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_ETH用AN8引脚采样，VCC50用AN1引脚采样。 CAN&ETH power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the output voltages VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 from the LP87702 and TPS22918. Both VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_ETH is sampled using AN8, and VCC50 is sampled using AN1.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F13</td></tr><tr><td>运行模式 Operation Mode</td><td>SHUTDOWN、READ OTP、STANDBY、ACTIVE</td></tr><tr><td>模式定义 Definition of Operation mode</td><td>SHUTDOWN NRST电压低于阈值。LP87702-Q1器件停止工作。 READ OTP 电源电压VANA高于VANAUVLO电平，并且NRST电压高于阈值电平。电压转换器被禁用，LP87702-Q1的参考电平和偏置电路被启用。OTP配置被加载到寄存器。 STANDBY 电源电压VANA高于VANAUVLO水平，NRST电压高于阈值水平。电压转换器被禁用，LP87702-Q1的参考电平、控制和偏压电路被启用。所有的寄存器都可以被主机处理器通过系统串口读写。电压转换器可以根据需要进行使能输出。 ACTIVE 电源电压VANA高于VANAUVLO水平，NRST电压高于阈值水平。稳压DC/DC变换器处于使能状态。所有的寄存器都可以被MCU通过系统串口读写。 SHUTDOWN After the enable signal MCU_OV75_MIPI_DDR_EN=L, the secondary power stops the 0V75_MIPI_DDR power supply. READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. ACTIVE The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The regulated DC/DC converter is in the enabled state. All the registers can be read and written by the MCU through the system serial port. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td>1、VANAUVLO阈值电压 2、NRST阈值电压 </td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | 唤醒电路 Wake Up circuit电源电路 power circuitKL30 EMI电路 KL30 EMI circuitKL30 转VCC3V3 KL30 to VCC3V3VCC3V3_MCU转VCC1V25_MCU VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCUVCC3V3转VCC0V75 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDRVCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50 VCC3V3 to VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQVCC3V3转VCC3V3_ETH VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETHVCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQTC377最小系统 TC377 minimum systemEQ6最小系统 EQ6 minimum system摄像头最小系统 camera minimum system看门狗电路 Watchdog circuit温度采样电路 Temp Sample circuit系统内电源电压采样电源 The power System Voltage Sampling circuitETH电路 ETH circuitCAN/CAN FD电路 CAN/CAN FD circuit3V3过压保护电路 3V3 overvoltage protection circuit硬件运行模式 Hardware Operation Mode主要用于描述当前ADAS硬件不同的运行模式（例如上电模式，下电模式，Sleep模式，运行模式等）。描述各个运行模式下的硬件模块功能，以及模式之间的切换条件。Describe the different operating mode of ADAS Hardware (e.g. power on mode, power off mode, Sleep mode，normal mode).
